# Eight famous cases — completed bounded snapshot

Saved evidence last fetched: 2026-10-23T20:50:42.759260Z. **1,147 of 1,148 ledger chain-address pairs have readable timelines.** Acquisition is complete for the bounded study; coverage limits remain.

Eight editorial cases are not eight entities: the SamSam case groups two OFAC-named individuals. 1 ledger pairs have no timeline, 0 files are unreadable/partial, and 0 changed during reading. Counts include explicit coverage stubs; a readable file is not necessarily a complete history.

Rebuild with `.venv/bin/python scripts/analysis/cases.py`. Verify with `.venv/bin/python scripts/analysis/cases.py --check`; run accounting/parser checks with `--self-test`. Everything runs offline. A changed input makes `--check` fail and requires a fresh rebuild; active fetches can legitimately invalidate the previous output.

The result wrapper records provisional status, timeline counts, input hashes and coverage. Its `cases` array follows the site contract and can be exported directly. Detail is retained in each case's `address_metrics`, `issuer_events`, `largest_post_T_inflows` and `findings`.

## Reading these timelines

- T is the chain-address first-publication date at 00:00 America/New_York; lag uses noon ET with full-day uncertainty interval.
- Every new/changed timeline present at inventory is parsed to EOF; identical previously audited bytes can reuse their SHA-256-matched syntax audit. Incomplete gzip/JSON and concurrent replacements are skipped and counted. Selected case files are always reanalysed; no fetched data is edited.
- Balance-at-T reconstruction requires complete paged histories, exact-asset reconciliation to current state, and exact timestamps. Missing observations are null.
- Four Tornado ETH pools use independent archive balances from the Tornado analysis, with their original block/time/raw references. Full listed set is used only for blacklist context.
- Recognized assets are native BTC/ETH/TRX/LTC and exact-contract USDT/USDC/DAI. All other token identities are unvalued, never silently priced by symbol.
- USD uses price.usd() at or before requested time, with the actual valuation timestamp and saved price raw reference. Today means each asset's latest saved state, not a live or atomic snapshot.
- First outflow is earliest observed successful positive transfer >=USD1 after that address's T. Incomplete histories cannot establish the true first outflow; UTXO amounts are net debits excluding allocated fee, not attributed output values.
- Largest inflows rank individual observed address net credits/asset transfers by USD at transfer time; they are not unique funds, verified payments or inferred sender identities.
- Blacklist event does not freeze native BTC/ETH/TRX. Blank frozen balances remain unknown; no present-day substitution. Lag is address-specific, including later additions.
- After T includes later delisting periods; Tornado post-2025-03-21 activity is not described as continued sanctioned use.
- Different designation-date cohorts are opening stocks at different times, not additive entity holdings; the same funds can move between listed addresses.
- Cases are selected for recognizability and saved trackable evidence, not a representative sample. Changes in behavior do not isolate a sanctions effect.

Research, not legal or compliance advice. On-chain receipts and transfers do not establish legal violations. Destination labels are leads, not ownership proof.

## Candidate findings

### hamas-tron-aljebouri-early-freeze

A Tron address later listed under AL-JEBOURI, Zaid Issam Ahmed was blacklisted by Tether 540.71 days before the noon-ET listing-date proxy.

Number: 540.7076736 **days**.

Confidence: High confidence in the cited saved on-chain records; coverage has documented limits. Date-level OFAC timing cannot establish same-day publication order. USD uses the stated saved observation, with stablecoin market-price assumptions inherited from the price source. The full lag interval is stored in the freeze event. Frozen amount is unknown; current balance is not substituted.

## TORNADO CASH

The protocol's contracts continued processing withdrawals; Treasury removed the designation in March 2025.

Within the saved scope, the first priced outflow after an address's listing was 0.1 ETH on 2022-08-08. Its order relative to OFAC's intraday publication is unknown. The matched issuer history begins with a USDC blacklist event on 2022-08-08. A blacklist record alone does not state how many tokens were immobilized. A later non-dust receipt is recorded on 2026-10-23; its sender's purpose is unknown. The four classic ETH contracts are separate from the full address set matched to issuer records. Use after the March 2025 removal is a distinct legal period.

[Treasury press release](https://home.treasury.gov/news/press-releases/jy0916) · [OFAC action](https://ofac.treasury.gov/recent-actions/20220808). Removal plus re-designation on 2022-11-08 is not a sanctions-free interval. Do not label post-2025-03-21 protocol use as post-designation sanctioned activity without a separate then-current basis.

Scope: 91 listed chain-address pairs; 4 analysed for balances/flows. four classic Ethereum pools; native coin and allowlisted stablecoins; no same-string activity on unlisted chains.

## LAZARUS GROUP

The address-addition dates differ from the group's original designation; the listed Ethereum addresses kept moving funds.

Within the saved scope, the first priced outflow after an address's listing was 2915.24 ETH on 2022-04-15. The matched issuer history begins with a USDC blacklist event on 2022-04-14. A blacklist record alone does not state how many tokens were immobilized. A later non-dust receipt is recorded on 2026-10-01; its sender's purpose is unknown.

[Treasury press release](https://home.treasury.gov/news/press-releases/jy0768) · [OFAC action](https://ofac.treasury.gov/recent-actions/20220414). The primary date is an address addition, not Lazarus Group's original designation. No same-day Treasury release is linked on the 2022 page; the May 6 Blender release explicitly recalls the April 14 addition.

Scope: 8 listed chain-address pairs; 8 analysed for balances/flows. OFAC-listed addresses in this case with readable timelines; native coin and allowlisted stablecoins; no same-string activity on unlisted chains.

[Source](https://home.treasury.gov/news/press-releases/jy0768). ## GARANTEX EUROPE OU

A later-listed address was blacklisted by Tether, unblacklisted, and then listed by OFAC.

Within the saved scope, the first priced outflow after an address's listing was 358003 USDT on 2022-04-05. Its order relative to OFAC's intraday publication is unknown. The matched issuer history begins with a USDC blacklist event on 2022-04-06. A blacklist record alone does not state how many tokens were immobilized. A later non-dust receipt is recorded on 2025-09-17; its sender's purpose is unknown.

[Treasury press release](https://home.treasury.gov/news/press-releases/jy0701) · [OFAC action](https://ofac.treasury.gov/recent-actions/20220405). March 2025 disruption is described in the August Treasury release; do not equate the initial 2022 listed addresses with the vendor's entire exchange cluster.

Scope: 12 listed chain-address pairs; 12 analysed for balances/flows. OFAC-listed addresses in this case with readable timelines; native coin and allowlisted stablecoins; no same-string activity on unlisted chains.

## SINBAD

A listed Bitcoin address was spent after the designation; the transaction does not establish who authorized it.

Within the saved scope, the first priced outflow after an address's listing was 0.35113163 BTC on 2024-02-16. The later Bitcoin debit does not identify the spender, and the coordinated disruption prevents a sanctions-only explanation.

[Treasury press release](https://home.treasury.gov/news/press-releases/jy1933) · [OFAC action](https://ofac.treasury.gov/recent-actions/20231129). OFAC's primary name is SINBAD; SINBAD.IO is an alias. A coordinated seizure also affected operations.

Scope: 2 listed chain-address pairs; 2 analysed for balances/flows. OFAC-listed addresses in this case with readable timelines; native coin and allowlisted stablecoins; no same-string activity on unlisted chains.
